# Seed 39 on the AMD Ryzen 7 PRO 8840HS - small again: the gene set has converged under the new selection (Iteration 168)

`seed 39 tag2 7h then experiment compare-fronts.py --rounds 6 --frontier
--cpus 2,8`, commit 5230fe0b (Iteration 153), started 21:38: the fronts of
seeds 35, 36, 37 and 38 carried in, population 256, 82 generations before the
deadline, 21,153 designs, 20,085 alive - the deaths the reach limit's at scale
0, as seed 38's. The chain took 6 h 28 min of its 7 hours: the run packed at
02:32, the comparison at 04:07 - 32 minutes to spare. Session 34 measured it
with seeds 35-38 (calibration 0.996): **small** - fastest 0.239 against seed
38's 0.237, smallest 20,622 bytes against 20,622, memory 152 KB against 152.
Seed 38 was small too: two in a row, the gene set has converged under the
selection with bubble and ackermann - the batch after it changes that
selection. 71 of the joint front's 119 designs are seed 39's, 39 seed 38's, 9
seed 37's; 74 of the 119 are JIT designs, the fastest (9932cc5c32, 0.237)
among them.

Against the predictions written before the run (results/predictions.md):
small - right; the front shared with seed 38 - right (71 and 39 of 119); the
fastest still not a JIT design - wrong, 9932cc5c32 is one; the chain ends on
time - right.

The run's report:

# Evolved VM designs

21153 designs evaluated, 20085 alive. Speed is the geometric mean of median cpu time / hand-made s6, long workloads (Iteration 100) over kernel, fib, corpus, loop, bubble, ackermann,
relative to s6-cv8b; size is the self-hosting image, binary the stripped engine. sieve, parse is held out.
The front is over three objectives, equal (Iteration 75): speed, binary + image, peak memory - memory within 8 KB a tie (Iteration 77).

## The front's genomes

Each design above, and any other the front was measured again with, as one JSON line: its record without the raw timings. A tool that takes a database (--db) reads this report as one - the lines that are not JSON are skipped - so results/ is all a new chat needs to rebuild a front design.
